### 2026-10-07 16:06:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

India’s FX Reserves Fall for Fourth Week — India’s foreign exchange reserves fell for a fourth consecutive week to $734.6 billion as of October 2, 2026, according to a footnote to Reserve Bank of India Governor Sanjay Malhotra’s monetary policy statement. Reserves have declined from their recent peak of $785.71 billion on September 4, a drop of around $50 billion in less than a month. The drawdown reflects RBI intervention in the foreign-exchange market, including dollar sales to smooth the rupee’s decline and sell/buy FX swaps aimed at absorbing surplus liquidity. Despite the central bank’s support, including its first rate hike since 2023 in October, the rupee has continued to weaken, pressured by elevated oil prices, rising US Treasury yields and increased foreign equity outflows.

### 2026-10-07 15:53:44  #公司 #焦點 #國際

【歐盟就G7釋放石油儲備達成共識：不強制新增釋放石油儲備】上週七國集團（G7）達成協議，計劃投放1億桶原油與柴油儲備。歐盟各國普遍達成共識，歐盟成員國沒有義務為此釋放新的石油儲備。據報道，備忘錄顯示，在週三上午歐盟成員國官員會議上，各方廣泛認同：後續釋放石油庫存，應當限定在今年3月已經商定的額度之內。檔案顯示，多個國家希望由國際能源署（IEA）開展影響評估。備忘錄稱，可以提前投放柴油儲備（這是G7協議的核心內容），但投放規模會比較有限；沒有任何歐盟國家明確承諾會在20天視窗期內完成儲備釋放。法國、義大利或考慮在3月既有承諾基礎之上額外釋放部分柴油，但尚未作出最終決定。駐布魯塞爾的法國、義大利發言人暫未回應置評請求。歐盟發言人也尚未回覆置評申請。國際能源署將於今日下午就此議題展開磋商。

FTSE MIB Ends 3-Day Winning Streak — The FTSE MIB fell over 1% to around 50,500 on Wednesday, ending a three-session winning streak and tracking broader European losses as investors remained cautious amid continued tensions in the Middle East. Oil prices rose amid escalating Saudi-Houthi tensions and a storm threat in the Gulf of Mexico, while higher bond yields also pressured sentiment. Banks led the decline, with BPER Banca down 1.9%, Banco BPM 1.7%, UniCredit 1%, Intesa Sanpaolo 0.9%, and MPS 0.8%. Industrials and technology also weighed, with Prysmian falling 2.7%, STMicroelectronics 1.6%, and Fincantieri 1.2%. On the flipside, gains were seen in Stellantis (+2%), Inwit (+1.9%), and Telecom Italia (+1.6%).

【眾生藥業：ZSP1601片納入突破性治療藥物程式】眾生藥業(002317.SZ)公告稱，公司控股子公司眾生睿創自主研發的一類創新藥物ZSP1601片被國家藥監局藥品審評中心納入突破性治療藥物程式，擬定適應症為代謝相關脂肪性肝炎(MASH)。ZSP1601片是具有全球自主智慧財產權、全新作用機制的治療MASH首創一類創新藥（First-in-Class），為國家重大新藥創制專案。ZSP1601片治療MASH的IIb期臨床試驗已獲得積極的資料結果，治療48周後達到了病理學改善的主要療效終點。同時實現抗纖維化、降低肝臟脂肪含量、改善肝酶的三重獲益，起效快、效果穩健、安全性優異。

### 2026-10-07 15:50:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Snaps 3-day Gain — Frankfurt’s DAX 40 fell 0.8% to around 25,240 in early trading on Wednesday, snapping a three-session winning streak, as a renewed rise in oil prices and elevated bond yields weighed on sentiment. Brent crude rose back above $100 a barrel as Iran stepped up attacks on tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz, fueling inflation concerns and raising the prospect of higher interest rates. Investors now await the Federal Reserve’s meeting minutes later in the day for clues on the path of US interest rates. Meanwhile, better-than-expected German industrial production provided some support, with output rising 2.0% month-on-month in August, well above expectations of a 0.5% increase, keeping hopes of an emerging economic recovery alive. On the corporate front, shares of Infineon fell 3.6%, while Siemens Energy declined 2.0%, Deutsche Bank dropped 1.3%, and Vonovia lost 1.3%.

French Stocks Hit Over 6-Month Low — The CAC 40 fell 0.7% to 7,808 on Wednesday, reversing the previous session’s gains and hitting its lowest level since March, as geopolitical tensions and concerns over France’s fiscal outlook weighed on sentiment. Oil prices rose as concerns over further Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia outweighed relief from signs of recovering Middle Eastern oil supplies. In France, as investors doubt whether the government can cut the deficit to 5.0% of GDP in 2027, ECB Governing Council member Emmanuel Moulin said the country’s bond-market situation is serious but does not warrant intervention. On the economic front, France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August from a revised €6.6 billion in July, beating expectations for a €6.5 billion shortfall. Exports fell 0.8% mom to €54.2 billion, while imports declined 1.5% to €60.3 billion. Most stocks traded lower, led by financials, with BNP Paribas (-1.2%), Crédit Agricole (-1.1%), and Société Générale (-1.6%) among the biggest drags.

### 2026-10-07 15:35:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Czech Trade Deficit Widens in August — The trade deficit in the Czech Republic widened to CZK 5.3 billion in August 2026 from CZK 0.3 billion surplus a year earlier, exceeding market expectations of CZK 4.4 billion. Imports rose 10.2% year-on-year to CZK 377.2 billion, driven mainly by higher purchases of crude petroleum and natural gas (+67.6%), coke and refined petroleum products (+47.9%), other transport equipment (+81.4%), and computers, electronic and optical products (+21.3%). Meanwhile, exports rose 8.5% year-on-year to CZK 371.9 billion driven largely by stronger sales of crude petroleum and natural gas (+871.0%), electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning (+100.3%), other transport equipment (+32.7%), and computers, electronic and optical products (+20.6%). In the January–August period, the trade surplus reached CZK 83.7 billion, down by CZK 42.8 billion a year earlier, as imports outpaced exports, rising 5.1% and 3.6%, respectively.

European Stocks Lower — European stocks fell on Wednesday, with the STOXX 50 down 0.6% and the STOXX 600 losing 0.4%, snapping a three-day winning streak. The rise in crude prices also pushed government bond yields higher, as investors remained concerned about the inflationary impact of elevated energy costs. Industrial stocks weakened as well, with Siemens down 1.8% and Siemens Energy falling 1.7%. ### 2026-10-07 15:25:01  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

India 10Y Yield Hits 2-Year High as RBI Tightens Policy — The yield on India’s 10-year G-Sec rose to around 7.23%, hitting more than two-year highs as investors weighed the RBI’s rate hike against persistent inflation risks, higher global bond yields, and elevated oil prices. The benchmark 6.94% 2036 bond yield gained 2 basis points, while the 10-year government bond yield later climbed 5 basis points after the RBI raised its repo rate by 25 basis points to 5.50%, the highest in a year and its first hike since February 2023. The move was in line with expectations and came as the central bank shifted its stance to “calibrated tightening,” with retail inflation reaching 4.8% in August after rising for 10 straight months. Markets are now watching for further liquidity tightening through additional debt sales or a modest increase in the cash reserve ratio. In September, the RBI sold INR 1 trillion ($10.39 billion) of bonds, the largest in at least a decade.

### 2026-10-07 15:20:40  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Construction Output #Importance 1

Czechia Construction Output Falls 1.4% in August — Construction output in the Czech Republic fell 1.4% year-on-year in August 2026, reversing a 1.8% increase in the previous month. Building construction rose 5.9% year-on-year, accelerating from 4.9% growth in July, while civil engineering declined further, falling 12.3% after a 3.4% drop. A total of 5,684 building permits were granted, up 1.4% from a year earlier, while the floor area of newly permitted buildings increased 20.2% year-on-year. Meanwhile, construction started on 3,922 dwellings, climbed 42.3% year-on-year, driven by increases in both family houses and multi-dwelling buildings. Meanwhile, 2,852 dwellings were completed, down 3.0% year-on-year. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, construction output fell 0.1% in August, reversing a 0.4% increase in July.

### 2026-10-07 15:17:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Seychelles #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Seychelles Inflation Highest Since 2024 — The annual inflation rate in Seychelles climbed to 1.22% in September 2026 from 0.93% in the previous month, marking the highest reading since December 2024. Inflation accelerated across all major groups, with fish prices rising 6.07% year-on-year (2.83% in August), while non-food and other food prices increased to 1.28% and 0.32%, respectively, from 1% and 0.28% in the previous month. Within non-food items, prices rose fastest for alcoholic beverages (5.25% vs 4.53%), transport (4.84% vs 3.46%), restaurants and hotels (3.43% vs 2.84%), and education (2.49% vs 2.21%). Other food prices were driven by vegetables (4.01% vs 3.85%), non-alcoholic beverages (2.77% vs 2.19%), and meat (1.98% vs 1.51%), while oils and fats eased to 3.79% from 4.65%. Meanwhile, annual core inflation rose to 1.24% from 0.97% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.24%, up from 0.18% in August.

France Current Account Deficit Smallest in 4 Months — France’s current account deficit narrowed to a seasonally adjusted €1.5 billion in August 2026 from a downwardly revised €3.9 billion in the previous month. It marked the smallest shortfall since April, driven by a narrower goods trade deficit, which fell to €5.3 billion from €6.8 billion, while the services surplus widened to €4.8 billion from €3.8 billion. Meanwhile, the primary income surplus was broadly unchanged at €4.2 billion, and the secondary income deficit remained steady at €5.1 billion. In the first eight months of 2026, France’s current account deficit narrowed to €10.3 billion from €17.2 billion in the corresponding period of 2025.

Czech Industrial Output at 3-Month Low — Industrial production in the Czech Republic increased 2.6% year-on-year in August 2026, below market expectations and the previous month's 3.1% growth. It marked the lowest level since May, as output growth in the electricity, gas, steam and air-conditioning supply sector eased sharply to 2.0% from 10.8% in July. Meanwhile, manufacturing activity edged up to 2.7% from 2.6%, supported by stronger production of wood and wood products (4.4% vs 2.0%) and paper and paper products (8.1% vs 0.6%). At the same time, the contraction in mining and quarrying eased significantly (-0.1% vs -11.9%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output rose 0.3% in August, rebounding from a 1.1% decline in the previous month.

### 2026-10-07 15:00:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

France Trade Gap Narrows More Than Expected — France’s trade deficit narrowed to €6.1 billion in August 2026 from a revised €6.6 billion in July, coming below the expected €6.5 billion shortfall. Exports fell 0.8% month-on-month to €54.2 billion, as shipments of transport equipment (-5.7%), agricultural, forestry and fishing products (-5.4%), and other industrial products (-0.4%) declined. This was partly offset by higher refined petroleum product exports (+10.5%) and mechanical, electrical, electronic and computer equipment (+2.6%). By destination, exports declined to Asia (-8.4%), the Middle East (-43.2%), and Africa (-6.3%), while increasing to the EU (+0.9%) and the Americas (+9.5%). Meanwhile, imports fell 1.5% to €60.3 billion, led by transport equipment (-3.9%), refined petroleum products and coke (-3.4%), and agricultural, forestry and fishing products (-1.8%). By origin, imports decreased from the EU (-1.2%), Asia (-0.5%), and the Middle East (-39.6%), while increasing from Africa (+6.5%) and the Americas (+1.3%).

The court found that planning authorities had failed to adequately account for emissions generated by customers who burn the mine’s coal. Minerals Council of Australia chief executive Tania Constable said the ruling could significantly undermine investment and send a negative signal to Australia’s trading and investment partners about sovereign risk, potentially putting future supply under pressure. Meanwhile, global coal demand is expected to reach a record this year as gas supplies face disruptions linked to the Iran war. Compared with a year earlier, industrial production climbed 2.3%, recovering from a 1.8% fall in July.

The decline was driven by weaker output in food, beverages and tobacco (-1.2% vs 0.4% in July), food products (-1.0% vs -0.1%), textiles, wearing apparel and leather (-0.9% vs 0.5%), refined petroleum, chemicals and pharmaceuticals (-1.7% vs -1.1%), computer and electrical equipment (-0.5% vs 2.2%), and machinery and equipment (-2.8% vs 5.7%). Output also increased in rubber, plastic and mineral products (3.8% vs 1.5%), basic metals (4.0% vs -2.6%), non-ferrous metals (3.4% vs -3.5%), fabricated metal products (3.5% vs 0.4%), and repair and installation of machinery (1.0% vs 1.8%). Still, a softer ringgit and firmer crude oil prices amid Middle East supply constraints helped limit losses by supporting palm oil’s appeal as a biodiesel feedstock. Meanwhile, China's Dalian markets will resume activity on Thursday.

### 2026-10-07 12:13:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

Indonesia Forex Reserves Slightly Fall in September — Indonesia’s forex exchange reserves slightly fell to USD 146.3 billion in September from a five-month high of USD 146.5 billion in the previous month. The decline was primarily due to government external loan withdrawals and tax and services receipts, amid repayments of maturing government foreign debt and Bank Indonesia’s rupiah stabilization measures in response to persistent uncertainty in global financial markets. Despite the decline, reserve assets remained robust, equivalent to 5.3 months of imports, or 5.2 months of imports and government external debt servicing, well above the international adequacy benchmark of around three months of imports. Looking ahead, Bank Indonesia expects external sector resilience to remain solid, supported by ample foreign exchange reserves and sustained foreign capital inflows, underpinned by positive investor sentiment toward Indonesia’s economic prospects and attractive investment returns.

Corn Futures Rise on US Harvest Concerns — Corn futures rose toward $5.1 per bushel, hitting a one-week high as concerns over delayed US harvesting and weaker crop conditions supported prices. Only 23% of the US corn crop had been harvested by October 4, below the 27% five-year average and 26% expected by analysts, after heavy Midwest rainfall delayed fieldwork. The share of the crop rated good-to-excellent also fell to 54% from 57% a week earlier. Meanwhile, export demand remains relatively firm, with 2026/27 shipments running 4.4% above last year at a record pace for this point in the marketing year, despite weekly inspections falling to 1.368 million tonnes. Elsewhere, Brazil’s first corn crop was 38% planted, slightly behind 40% a year earlier. Traders now await Friday’s USDA report for updated production and yield estimates, with current forecasts at 178.5 bushels per acre and 15.8 billion bushels of output.

【Shenzhen property market during National Day: price no longer decisive】Field checks across Shenzhen's Longgang and Longhua districts during the National Day holiday show developers stepping up incentives—appliance giveaways, discounts and cash rebates—while viewing volumes remain roughly in line with prior years. Li Yujia, chief researcher at Guangdong Housing Policy Research Center, says Shenzhen is likely experiencing a structural 'Golden October'—transaction volumes may rebound sequentially but a simultaneous rise in prices is unlikely; demand will concentrate in high-value, well-located, high-quality projects, while a shortage of standout inventory versus abundant average stock persists.

US Natural Gas Prices Extend Gains — US natural gas prices climbed to around $3.13 per MMBtu, extending gains for a fourth session and reaching their highest level since September 25, supported by falling production, LNG feedgas flows, and expectations of more demand. Average gas output in the US Lower 48 states fell to 111.7 bcfd so far in October, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in both August and September, while daily production continued to drop partly due to recent force majeure events and other disruptions affecting pipelines in Kentucky, Texas, and West Virginia. ### 2026-10-07 10:46:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Fiji #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Fiji Inflation Eases From 15-Year High — Fiji’s annual inflation slowed to 6.8% in September 2026 from 7.6% in the previous month, which was the highest since December 2011. The moderation came as food inflation eased to 7.6% from a 28-month high of 9.5% in August, while housing inflation eased to a four-month low of 4.9% from 7.0% in August. Inflation also moderated for miscellaneous goods and services (3.1% vs 3.6%) and furnishings, household equipment, and routine household maintenance (0.1% vs 0.4%), while remaining flat for clothing and footwear (0.0% vs. 1.2%). Meanwhile, inflation accelerated for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (13.9% vs 13.3%) and transport (12.2% vs 11.7%), while education inflation was steady at 0.4%. Additionally, recreation and culture prices fell at a faster pace (-1.5% vs -1.4%). On a monthly basis, the CPI fell 0.4% in September, following a 0.1% decline in August and marking the third straight monthly decline.

Soybeans Rebound on Brazil Planting Delays — Soybean futures climbed toward $13.1 per bushel, bouncing off from multi-week lows as slower planting in Brazil and signs of continued export demand lifted prices. About 7.3% of soybean crop in Brazil, the world’s largest exporter, had been planted as of Thursday, below 9% a year earlier, with consultancy AgRural estimating 2026/27 production at 182.7 million metric tons. The USDA has a higher forecast of 186 million tons, which would exceed last year’s record 180.5 million tons. On the demand side, an unnamed buyer purchased 104,000 tonnes of US soybeans, while US export inspections reached 3.99 million tonnes since the start of September, up from 3.05 million tonnes a year earlier. However, gains remained limited by expectations for a large US crop and weak Chinese demand. China’s soybean purchases are being constrained by high inventories, poor crushing margins and an additional 10% tariff on US soybeans, while private buyers continue to favor supplies from Brazil and Argentina.

Meanwhile, markets are pricing in around an 80% chance that the Federal Reserve will leave rates unchanged this month following a weaker-than-expected labor market report. Platinum’s fundamentals also remain mixed, with WPIC forecasting industrial demand to rise 5% in 2026, partly driven by AI infrastructure, while automotive demand is expected to fall 4%, leaving a projected 265,000-ounce surplus. ### 2026-10-07 10:15:54  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Analyst: Risk balance currently favors oil holding near $100/bbl】Analyst Eamonn Sheridan says a Gulf of Mexico storm is forecast to become the first Atlantic hurricane of 2026 within two days and may strike oil and gas infrastructure. Offshore areas along the storm track produce about 15% of US crude and 5% of US gas; as many as six refineries could be affected. US refining capacity is roughly 18 mln bpd, about half in Gulf Coast states, so fuel supply risk from the storm could match crude disruption; diesel prices are at historic highs. Middle East export flows had improved but recent escalation offsets that: Saudi airport was hit twice Monday evening and fighting between Saudi forces and Iran-backed Houthi fighters has intensified, with Saudi-backed Yemeni government forces advancing backed by Saudi air strikes. Diplomatic signals offer little relief. Sheridan assesses the risk balance is more supportive of oil remaining near $100/bbl than declining, unless the storm weakens or Middle East tensions ease.

Gasoline Rises for Second Day — US gasoline futures rose above $3.30 per gallon, gaining for the second straight session, as a storm in the Gulf of Mexico threatened to disrupt refinery operations. Refineries in US Gulf states account for about half of US refining capacity, which stands at 18.2 million barrels per day. Meanwhile, continued attacks in the Middle East kept energy-market risks elevated despite improvement in regional shipments. Russia’s ongoing restrictions on diesel exports and refinery disruptions, alongside China’s reported suspension of some October fuel exports, also tightened fuel markets. Gasoline inventories were 7% below the five-year average as of September 25, according to the EIA, while refinery utilization stood at 92.5%. The EIA also raised its 2026 and 2027 oil price forecasts, citing rapidly falling global inventories and supply risks. Separately, the G7’s planned release of emergency crude and diesel reserves could provide some relief.

Meanwhile, the Aussie found some support as the US dollar weakened broadly, with its biggest decline against the euro as stress in European bond markets eased. In Australia, consumer sentiment slumped for a second straight month in October as higher borrowing costs squeezed household finances and added to cost-of-living pressures. Attention will also turn to the RBA’s meeting minutes next week for further policy signals.

South Korean Shares Extend Decline on Chip Outlook — The benchmark KOSPI edged lower to around 6,910 on Wednesday, extending losses from the previous session as concerns over the sustainability of the semiconductor earnings boom and elevated global bond yields weighed on sentiment. Meanwhile, oil prices fell amid recovering Middle Eastern exports and a planned G7 release of strategic reserves. South Korea's stronger coverage of November crude oil supplies also reduced concerns over potential energy disruptions.

【A$70 billion Australian coal sector faces new climate-law risk after High Court ruling】Australia’s High Court on Wednesday ruled in a major coal mine expansion case that a local planning authority failed to properly consider measures to limit greenhouse gas emissions. A lower court had found scope 3 emissions from customers burning the coal would account for about 98% of the project’s potential pollution. The decision will directly affect New South Wales coal mine approvals and could serve as a precedent for similar projects nationwide; roughly 30 new or expanded coal projects are seeking government sign-off. Australia’s coal export sector is about A$70 billion (US$49 billion); miners warn approval delays could hit jobs and damage Australia’s standing as an energy supplier.

Silver Slips as Oil Prices Rebound — Silver slipped below $61 an ounce on Wednesday, paring gains from the previous session as oil prices rebounded on persistent risks to Middle East supply, keeping inflationary risks and rate hike concerns in focus. Treasury yields also remained near multi-decade highs amid concerns over persistent inflation, rising fiscal risks and elevated AI-related debt issuances. Investors now await minutes from the Fed’s latest meeting for further clues on the monetary policy outlook.

### 2026-10-07 09:14:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Philippines Manufacturing Output Growth Picks Up in August — Manufacturing production in the Philippines increased by 10.8% year-on-year in August, accelerating from an upwardly revised 9.9% rise in the previous month. The largest positive contribution came from the manufacture of computer, electronic and optical products (30.1% vs 15.8%), which accounted for 57.3% of the acceleration in the annual growth rate. Other major contributors included the manufacture of transport equipment (11.5% vs 4.9%), basic metals (27.2% vs 23.9%), rubber and plastic products (15.0% vs 9.9%), electrical equipment (10.9% vs 2.9%), and beverages (4.2% vs 3.6%). Meanwhile, production growth moderated for coke and refined petroleum products (24.7% vs 47.6%) and food products (1.3% vs 2.6%). For the January-August period, manufacturing output was 9.3% higher than in the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-07 09:14:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Philippines Jobless Rate Falls to 5.3% — The unemployment rate in the Philippines fell to 5.3% in August 2026 from a four-year high of 6% in the previous month. The number of unemployed persons declined to 2.77 million from 3.14 million, while the employment rate rose to 94.7% from 94% and the number of employed persons increased to 49.79 million from 49.21 million over the same period. The underemployment rate also eased to 11.9% from 12.9%, with 5.90 million workers seeking additional hours or another job. On a year-on-year basis, however, the unemployment rate remained above 3.9% in August 2025, while the employment rate was below 96.1% and the number of employed persons was down from 50.10 million. The underemployment rate was also higher than 10.7% a year earlier. The services sector remained the largest employer, accounting for 61.7% of total employment, followed by agriculture at 20.9% and industry at 17.3%. Average weekly working hours fell to 39.2 from 40.6 in July and 41.0 a year earlier.

### 2026-10-07 09:13:25  #宏觀 #市場 #國際

【貝森特執掌下的美國財政部：內部緊張、人事動盪及未達成的經濟目標】據媒體報道，知情人士稱，在1月財政部的一場會議上，美國財長貝森特當面質問負責恐怖主義與金融情報事務的財政部副部長約翰・赫爾利，當著多名下屬的面大聲斥責，口出穢言。貝森特音量極大，附近辦公的員工都能聽見。訊息人士表示，幾周之後，貝森特便和幕僚商議，打算為這位已經獲得參議院確認任職的赫爾利另尋一份財政部之外的崗位。赫爾利後來調任美國駐經濟合作與發展組織大使。彼時，財長正面臨職業生涯中最嚴峻的挑戰，赫爾利的離職也成了財政部高層一連串人事出走中的一例。知情人士稱，貝森特的行事風格是多名核心官員離職的誘因，有時甚至是最主要原因。據十餘名財政部現任及前任官員透露，自2025年初就任財長以來，無論事情大小，貝森特既斥責高階顧問，也訓斥基層工作人員。但特朗普反而進一步倚重貝森特，授權他去化解多項難題，包括借貸成本高企、受能源價格推動而難以遏制的通脹，以及政府債務快速累積的困境。與此同時，貝森特還受命透過一項名為“經濟棄兒行動”的計劃，以經濟手段終結對伊朗的戰爭。一名曾與貝森特共事的政府高階官員表示，總統整套經濟政策的重擔全部壓在這位財長身上。該官員稱：“斯科特輸不起。”貝森特曾建議特朗普推行“3-3-3”政策：到2028年將預算赤字降至國內生產總值的3%、推動國內生產總值實現3%增長、每日增產300萬桶石油。但實際經濟增速僅約2%，自特朗普上任以來石油日增產量不足100萬桶。美國負責任聯邦預算委員會資料顯示，當前預算赤字佔國內生產總值比重超過6%，美國債務總量近期突破40萬億美元。

【China's railways expect 24.15 million passengers today】China's railways expect to carry 24.15 million passengers today, the final day of the National Day–Mid-Autumn holiday, after return flows rose from yesterday. 【油氣運輸量上升之際 伊朗對霍爾木茲海峽油輪的襲擊愈發頻繁】近日，隨著經由全球最重要能源咽喉要道的石油運輸量接近戰前水平，伊朗加快了對霍爾木茲海峽油輪的襲擊步伐。英國海上貿易行動辦公室報告稱，本月該水道已發生9起襲擊事件，佔9月份該辦公室在霍爾木茲海峽和波斯灣地區報告襲擊總數的一半。上個月的資料因最後兩天的4起襲擊而有所增加，這凸顯了近期襲擊的加速趨勢。在美伊戰爭的大部分時間裡，霍爾木茲海峽的船隻一直遭到襲擊，但強度不一。此前幾輪猛烈打擊曾導致運輸量短暫下降，不過這些下降通常會在隨後幾天因運輸量增加而得到彌補。海事安全官員和航運高管表示，目前尚不清楚最近的局勢升級是否會導致霍爾木茲海峽的運輸量下降。近幾周海上天然氣運輸量也有所上升，其受到的影響仍不確定。

On an annual basis, private house approvals jumped 18.4%, picking up from an 8.2% increase in July.

### 2026-10-07 08:34:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Building Permits MoM #Importance 1

Steep Decline in Australia Building Permits Confirmed — Australia’s seasonally adjusted dwelling approvals fell 6.1% month-on-month to a seven-month low of 16,953 units in August 2026, matching the flash estimate and deepening from a 1.9% decline in the previous month. It was the fifth time of contraction so far this year, with approvals for private-sector dwellings excluding houses plummeting (-21.2% vs 0.8% in July) to 5,674 units. In contrast, private-sector house approvals rebounded (3.7%vs -1.9%) to 10,885 units. Across the states, dwelling approvals declined in Queensland (-22.5%), New South Wales (-17.3%) and Tasmania (-1.5%), but increased in South Australia (24.0%), Victoria (8.9%) and Western Australia (3.2%). On a yearly basis, building approvals rose 10.3%, easing slightly from a 10.9% gain in July.

### 2026-10-07 07:56:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 1

Japan Forex Reserves Fall Further in September — Japan’s official reserve assets fell by USD 29.1 billion, or 2.4%, to USD 1.178 trillion at the end of September 2026 from USD 1.208 trillion a month earlier, extending the decline following August’s record drop. The decrease was led by foreign currency reserves, which fell to USD 976.9 billion from USD 995.0 billion, with securities declining to USD 821.7 billion from USD 839.6 billion. Deposits with foreign central banks and the Bank for International Settlements edged down to USD 154.8 billion from USD 155.0 billion. Gold reserves also fell to USD 113.6 billion from USD 124.1 billion, while SDR holdings declined to USD 60.8 billion and the IMF reserve position to USD 11.3 billion. Other reserve assets stood at USD 15.8 billion. The latest decline follows the record USD 79.6 billion drop in August after Japan’s ¥15.4 trillion yen-buying intervention to support the currency.

【必和必拓將已關停的澳大利亞鎳礦廠出售給金田公司】必和必拓集團將把位於西澳的坎巴爾達（Kambalda）鎳選礦廠及配套土地出售給金田有限公司（Gold Fields Ltd.），交易金額未對外披露。必和必拓週三釋出宣告稱，金田公司將 “評估這座選礦廠的長期用途”。本次交易包含採礦租約與礦權。受全球鎳供應過剩、價格暴跌影響，必和必拓於 2024 年年中關停坎巴爾達選礦廠，同步暫停整個西澳鎳業務運營。必和必拓表示，公司仍在評估整體鎳業務；鎳精煉廠、冶煉廠及兩座大型礦山等其餘資產的處置決議預計將於 2027 年 2 月出臺。受印尼這種電池金屬大量增產拖累鎳價下行，這家全球最大礦業集團已於 2024 年對其鎳資產計提 25 億美元減值損失。金田公司一直在該區域物色金礦基礎設施與礦山資產。該公司目前正考慮對北極星資源發起第二輪收購要約，北極星在坎巴爾達附近持有多個大型金礦專案。

### 2026-10-07 07:52:25  #MKT News

【Japan real wages up 1.5% YoY in August, bolstering BOJ tightening case】Japan's real wages rose 1.5% YoY in August, marking an eighth consecutive monthly increase, the Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare said, supporting the Bank of Japan's case for further rate hikes after last month's policy-rate increase. The August real-wage gain was below July's revised 2.0% increase. Average nominal wages rose 3.8% YoY in August (July revised 4.3%); base pay +3.8% YoY (unchanged from revised July); overtime pay +5.2% (July revised 4.5%). The MHLW used a 2.2% inflation rate to calculate real wages, unchanged from July and below last year's 3.1%. Tokyo's September core CPI rose to a 10-month high last week, reinforcing upward price pressure and the case for additional BOJ tightening.

### 2026-10-07 07:36:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Wage Growth #Importance 1

Japan Nominal Wage Growth Tops Forecast — Average cash earnings in Japan increased by 3.8% year-on-year in August 2026, slightly exceeding market expectations for a 3.7% gain but slowing from a revised 4.3% rise in July. Regular pay, including base salaries, rose 3.8% to mark continued solid wage growth, while overtime pay accelerated to 5.2% from a revised 4.5% increase in July. In contrast, special payments, which mainly consist of one-time bonuses, were unchanged after a revised 5.3% increase in the previous month. Meanwhile, real wages rose 1.5%, marking the eighth consecutive month of gains, although growth eased from a revised 2.0% increase in July. The result was based on a 2.2% inflation rate, unchanged from July and below 3.1% a year earlier. The sustained real wage gains strengthen the case for the Bank of Japan to continue raising interest rates after its latest hike.

【美國券商Revere支付80萬美元和解監管指控】紐約券商Revere Securities LLC同意支付80萬美元，就監管機構提出的相關指控達成和解。監管機構稱，數十家總部大多設在亞洲的小企業首次公開募股(IPO)後，該公司未能識別涉及股價劇烈波動的疑似“拉高出貨”交易。美國金融業監管局(FINRA)週二指稱，Revere在協助這些企業進入美國資本市場的過程中，沒有對客戶進行充分審查，也未落實適當的反洗錢保障措施。此次執法行動是美國監管機構更廣泛打擊“拉高出貨”騙局的一部分。此類騙局通常由操縱者買入價格低廉、成交稀少的小公司股票，再透過網路論壇或聊天室吸引投資者買入，推高股價，隨後拋售持股，導致股價暴跌。Revere並未被指控操縱股價。該公司既未承認也未否認美國金融業監管局的指控，並同意聘請第三方顧問審查其內部政策。

Australia Industry Activity Slumps — Australia’s Industry Index plunged to -24.2 in September from a revised -1.6 in the previous month, underscoring mounting uncertainty as volatile prices and market conditions stifle investment, orders, and planning. Firms reported growing difficulty accessing finance, while cost pressures stayed high amid a second wave of fuel and downstream price rises that reignited inflation. The activity/sales indicator collapsed 28.5 points to -31.0, returning to deep contraction, and employment fell back into negative territory at -15.6, erasing August’s gains. New orders fell 22.4 points to -35.9, reversing prior gain, while input volumes weakened to -12.9. Input prices eased 10.6 points to 56.8, yet sales prices rose 13.0 points to 13.6, hinting at improved cost pass-through. The gap between input and sales prices remained wide at 43.2 points, leaving margins under strain despite partial relief. Capacity utilisation eased to 77.2% despite rising costs, labor shortages, and intense competition.

### 2026-10-07 06:44:48  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Industry Index Manufacturing #Importance 1

Australia Manufacturing Contraction Eases Sharply — Australia’s Ai Group Industry Index for manufacturing remained in contraction in September, albeit at a softer pace, rising to -8.7 from August’s revised -14.9, marking the highest level since May 2023. The sector remained under pressure as lower domestic demand, rising input costs, and wage pressures affected both customers and businesses, contributing to delayed investment decisions and softer orders. Upstream industries continued to contract, with companies reporting rising costs for materials, freight, utilities, and fuel, as well as weaker demand, increased competition, and Middle East shipping disruptions. Meanwhile, metals faced softer orders, import competition, and regulatory constraints. Downstream, machinery manufacturers reported improvements, with machinery companies reporting rising demand, while food and beverage manufacturers also registered stronger orders. Other industries reported rising inventories and higher utility costs.

【美債短暫反彈難改看空情緒 交易員押注收益率繼續攀升】長期美債收益率徘徊在24年高位附近之際，交易員進一步加碼做空美國國債，顯示儘管就業增長大幅放緩釋放出經濟降溫訊號，市場仍押注美債拋售將會持續。上週五疲軟的就業資料公佈後，一些交易員迅速回補空頭倉位，推動美債出現短暫反彈。但此後與長期美債掛鉤的未平倉合約持續增加，顯示交易員仍在為債券價格進一步下跌做準備。隨著收益率走高，10年期美債期貨未平倉合約在過去18個交易日中有14日增加，這種情況通常與空頭倉位增加有關。花旗策略師David Bieber表示，市場對美債的倉位仍處於“極端做空”水平，顯示“持續的做空傾向”依然存在。他表示，近期交易活動既受到新建空頭倉位推動，也受到美債多頭平倉影響。這些交易跡象表明，週二的行情可能只是持續數月的美債跌勢中的短暫喘息。儘管收益率小幅下降，但10年期和30年期美債收益率僅略低於週一觸及的高點。

US Crude Inventories Fall 2.09 Million Barrels — US crude oil inventories fell by 2.09 million barrels in the week ending October 2, after rising 1.019 million barrels in the previous week. Commercial crude inventories excluding the SPR have declined by more than 38 million barrels over the past 25 weeks, while total US crude inventories are up nearly 13 million barrels year to date. Another 800,000 barrels were released from the SPR, bringing total holdings to 283 million barrels. The generally accepted operational minimum for the SPR is between 250 million and 300 million barrels. Cushing inventories rose by 866,000 barrels. Gasoline inventories fell by 1.37 million barrels after rising 2.991 million barrels the previous week, while distillate inventories increased by 461,000 barrels following a 286,000-barrel decline. US oil production rose to 13.955 million bpd in the week ending September 25, from 13.939 million bpd a week earlier.

TSX Nears One-Year High as Yields Retreat — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose 0.4% to close at 35,649 on Tuesday, nearing a one-year high as the recent global bond sell-off eased. Yields retreated from multi-year highs, offering some respite to credit-sensitive markets. RBC gained 0.6%, TD Bank added 0.5%, BMO rose 0.7% and CIBC advanced 0.9%. Utilities also gained as lower yields increased the appeal of high-dividend-paying stocks. Miners advanced as gold prices rebounded, with Agnico Eagle up 1.2% and Barrick adding 1.6%. Meanwhile, Emera (-2.9%) said it would acquire Canadian Utilities (-1.9%) in a deal that would create one of Canada’s largest power providers. 【Shipowners pay heavy premiums for Strait of Hormuz transits; crew likened to mercenaries】Financial Times reports that as Iran has stepped up attacks on vessels, Gulf tanker captains can earn about $100k/month plus a $50k bonus per voyage for transits of the Strait of Hormuz, according to three sources close to owners and crew. Shipowners are offering large hazard allowances to persuade seafarers to remain aboard while Gulf states seek to keep crude moving through the choke point. Normal wages are about $1,500/month for ordinary crew and ~$15,000 for captains; pay typically doubles in the southern Red Sea and Gulf of Oman and rises further for each Hormuz transit, with ordinary crew receiving at least 4-6x their normal pay. One source said the seafarers are 'almost treated like mercenaries.'

The minister set two red lines: keep the deficit below 5% of GDP and avoid measures that would harm growth. French borrowing costs have risen and investors worry political deadlock could hinder public-spending control. If 49.3 is used deputies may table a no-confidence motion; if the budget is not finally voted within 70 days the government can adopt it by ordinance.

Participants in its "Project Glasswing"—US government agencies, financial institutions and large software vendors—will receive access; new entrants require joint ANTHROPIC–US government review. Certified defensive cybersecurity teams may use the models for malware reverse engineering and incident response.

### 2026-10-07 03:34:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Colombia PPI Growth Accelerates in September — Colombia’s Producer Price Index rose 2.20% year over year in September 2026, accelerating from a 0.76% increase in August. Mining and quarrying (6.38%) and agriculture, livestock, forestry, hunting and fishing (4.57%) recorded increases above the 2.20% average, while manufacturing rose 0.52%. The largest price increases were recorded for potatoes (155.92%), citrus fruits (34.58%) and crude petroleum oils and oils obtained from bituminous minerals (10.42%). In contrast, the main deflationary pressures came from coffee (-32.36%), milled or green coffee (-19.33%) and ethyl alcohol and other alcohols, denatured alcohols of any concentration (-27.51%).

### 2026-10-07 02:18:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Brazil Trade Surplus Surges in September — Brazil’s trade surplus surged 146.4% year over year to $7.74 billion in September 2026, above forecasts of $7.19 billion and marking the sharpest annual increase since January 2024. Exports rose 12.9% to $34.42 billion. Agriculture exports increased 4.8%, led by higher sales of rye, oats and other unmilled cereals (31,239.5%), unroasted coffee (8.7%) and soybeans (11.9%). Extractive industry shipments jumped 39.8%, driven by other crude minerals (104.4%), copper ores and concentrates (74.2%) and crude petroleum oils (77.3%). Manufacturing exports rose 5.3%, led by soybean meal and other animal feed, meat and animal flours (47.7%), fuel oils from petroleum or bituminous minerals (39.1%) and non-monetary gold. Imports fell 2.4% to $26.68 billion, driven by a 3.2% decline in manufacturing purchases, while agriculture imports rose 9.3% and extractive industry imports increased 7.4%.

Brent Back to $100 — Brent pared most of its early losses to trade around $100.1 a barrel on Tuesday, after falling to $97.8 earlier in the session as supply concerns resurfaced. A Saudi-led coalition said it had intercepted and destroyed a ballistic missile launched by the Houthis toward Khamis Mushait in Saudi Arabia, while reports indicated that Iran had stepped up attacks on tankers transiting the Strait of Hormuz in the past days. However, signs of improving supply flows continued to weigh on prices. Saudi Arabia’s Minister of Energy, Industry and Mineral Resources announced that the East-West oil pipeline had restored its crude pumping capacity to 5.8 million bpd. Meanwhile, G7 countries agreed on Friday to release 100 million barrels of diesel and crude from emergency reserves and pledged to refrain from imposing energy export restrictions. Despite these measures, the EIA projected Brent crude prices at $105 a barrel in Q4 2026, $14 higher than its previous forecast.

European Stocks Extend Rebound — European stocks closed firmly higher on Tuesday with support from banks, consumer discretionary companies, and auto producers as sovereign yields paused their recent surge. The Euro STOXX 50 added 0.5% to 6,274 and the STOXX Europe 600 gained 0.5% to 636.6. Long-maturity euro-area sovereign yields were mostly lower on the session, especially in economies with higher debt pressure, softening concerns that the ECB would be forced to intervene against fragmentation. Banks tracked the rebound in credit, with BNP Paribas jumping 2.5% while UniCredit, Santander, and Deutsche Bank added 1.5% each. Intesa Sanpaolo also gained 1.3%, rebounding from yesterday's drop as markets reconsidered how a potential acquisition of Banca MPS could impact future returns. On the other hand, Schneider Electric sank 4.5% after agreeing to buy US software firm PTC in a $22.6 billion deal.

German Stocks at Nearly 2-Week High — The DAX closed 0.8% higher at 25,449 on Tuesday, extending gains for a third consecutive session and reaching its highest level in around two weeks. The index tracked a broadly positive mood across European and US markets, supported by lower oil prices and bond yields. Concerns over France’s fiscal position remained a headwind for the broader market, however, alongside political uncertainty surrounding a snap election in Spain. Expectations for further ECB rate hikes have also receded, with markets now pricing in a nearly 80% probability of another rate increase by the end of the year. SAP (2.2%), Infineon (1.9%), Siemens (1.5%) and Siemens Energy (1.4%) posted strong gains, while Rheinmetall (-2.8%) was the worst performer on the DAX.

### 2026-10-06 23:47:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #France #Stock Market #Importance 0

CAC 40 Rebounds as Oil Prices and Bond Yields Ease — The CAC 40 rose 0.4% to 7,865 on Tuesday, rebounding from the previous session amid easing oil prices and a pullback in global bond yields. Oil prices fell as resilient Middle Eastern crude exports and a G7 emergency stockpile release eased supply concerns, reducing energy-driven inflation pressures. Yields also eased from multi-year highs, offering some respite to credit-sensitive markets. Financials posted solid gains, with BNP Paribas up 2.5% and AXA adding 1.5%. Luxury and industrial stocks were mixed as concerns over France’s high debt levels and political uncertainty lingered. The government sought to ease market jitters by unveiling its 2027 budget. The state budget deficit widened to €159.6 billion in January-August from €157.5 billion a year earlier. LVMH added 1%, while Hermes shed 0.5%. Among industrials, STMicroelectronics gained 2.5%, tracking strength in US chipmakers. Schneider Electric lost 3.8%, extending Monday’s losses after announcing its acquisition of PTC.

【Bowman: Fed to reshape bank supervision and reassess asset-size thresholds】The Federal Reserve plans a comprehensive overhaul of U.S. bank supervision, shifting oversight away from regional Reserve Bank presidents toward a Washington-led structure, Vice Chair for Supervision Michelle Bowman said on Tuesday. The proposal would create five supervisory geographic regions, each led by a regional head accountable for all supervisory activity in that region, while examination work would remain performed by regional Reserve Bank staff. Bowman said the change aims to strengthen accountability and clarify decision authority. The Fed will also consider later this year adjusting the asset-size thresholds that trigger stricter regulatory requirements.

### 2026-10-06 22:43:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #World #Supply Chain Pressure Index #Importance 1

Global Supply Chain Pressures Rise in September — The Global Supply Chain Pressure Index rose to 1.28 in September 2026, from an upwardly revised 1.20 in August, indicating a modest increase in supply chain pressures last month. Despite the rise, pressures remain well below their most recent peak of 1.81 in May and are far from the 4.43 reading recorded in December 2021, when widespread disruptions from the COVID-19 pandemic severely strained global supply chains. Elevated supply chain pressures can contribute to higher inflation by increasing costs and limiting the availability of goods. The latest increase comes as the Federal Reserve raised its interest-rate target last month and signaled another hike by year-end, as policymakers seek to bring inflationary pressures back toward the Fed’s target.

【歐洲央行管委科赫爾：能源價格上漲對預測構成風險】歐洲央行管理委員會成員馬丁·科赫爾（Martin Kocher）表示，近期能源市場的再次上行，對9月份釋出的奧地利及歐元區通脹預測構成了風險。週二，他在奧地利金融市場監管局（FMA）的一場會議上表示：“與此同時，能源價格再次小幅上漲。這意味著，通脹走高以及經濟增速可能放緩的風險再次增加。”由於伊朗戰爭及柴油短缺導致能源成本上升，歐洲央行決策者一直在評估這些成本是否正在向歐元區經濟的其他領域傳導，並以此判斷是否需要採取進一步措施，以確保通脹在中期內回落至2%的目標水平。今年已經兩次加息後，知情人士上月對彭博社透露，官員們預計利率將進一步上調，不過任何行動都將取決於即將公佈的資料。包括德國央行行長約阿希姆·納格爾和芬蘭央行行長奧利·雷恩在內的同僚近期表示，目前尚未出現通脹影響薪資的跡象。科赫爾表示，總體而言，歐元區經濟對地緣政治衝擊和高企的能源價格展現出了韌性，但不確定性正越來越多地影響到金融市場。科赫爾稱：“地緣政治緊張局勢、財政挑戰以及市場可能出現的急劇調整，依然是核心風險因素。”

【美債收益率脫離2002年以來高點 受油價下跌及貝森特講話影響】由於油價回落至每桶100美元下方，加之美國財政部長貝森特堅持認為政府債務負擔可控，美債收益率從2002年以來最高水平回落。貝森特試圖安撫投資者，稱經濟增長與支出約束相結合將“非常迅速地”開始改變政府舉債路徑。他週一晚間在賓夕法尼亞州參加爐邊談話時表示，政府將開始扭轉這一趨勢。10年期美債收益率下降2個基點至5.28%，兩年期收益率也下降約2個基點至4.8%。週二，隨著跡象顯示更多供應正透過霍爾木茲海峽，原油價格回落。對美國財政路徑的擔憂一直令投資者保持警惕。橋水創始人達利歐警告稱，美國正接近債務週期的極限，如果支出繼續超過收入，未來三年內可能面臨危機。他表示，美債還容易受到中國和日本購債需求下降的衝擊。不過，投資者懷疑貝森特能否在短期內顯著緩解財政壓力，也不願就此判斷債券拋售已經結束。

### 2026-10-06 22:19:23  #公司 #市場

【IMF：對沖基金快速擴張或放大市場風險】國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）在6日公佈的《全球金融穩定報告》第二章中指出，應警惕對沖基金可能對宏觀金融穩定帶來的風險。報告說，全球對沖基金資產規模增速過快，在資本市場波動中容易放大價格錯位和流動性緊張，一旦遭遇嚴重負面衝擊，可能引發系統性風險。《全球金融穩定報告》第二章通常聚焦於特定領域的結構性問題或前沿金融風險，一般先於《全球金融穩定報告》釋出。報告說，全球對沖基金資產規模從2013年的4萬億美元增長至2026年早些時候的13萬億美元。雖然在非銀行金融投資機構中的佔比僅約為5%，但自全球金融危機以來，對沖基金資產增速顯著快於其他大多數非銀行金融板塊。IMF指出，對沖基金資產快速擴張的同時，槓桿融資也成為其擴大投資規模的重要方式。所謂槓桿，是指透過借入資金等方式，以較少的自有資金進行更大規模投資。槓桿可以放大收益，但也會放大損失，使金融機構在市場下跌時更容易受到衝擊。當市場出現劇烈波動時，高槓杆可能迫使對沖基金迅速出售資產以滿足融資方的保證金要求或償還債務。如果大量基金同時減持資產，可能進一步壓低資產價格、加劇市場流動性緊張，從而放大市場波動。此外，對沖基金與大型交易商銀行等金融機構存在廣泛的融資、交易和衍生品業務聯絡。如果對沖基金遭遇嚴重虧損甚至違約，相關風險可能透過這些業務關係傳導至銀行和其他金融市場參與者，並進一步影響金融體系穩定。（新華社）

【數十年高位收益率凸顯債券吸引力 Pimco強調分散配置】Pimco表示，債券收益率處於數十年來高位，為投資者提供了頗具吸引力的收益機會，投資者應將資產配置分散至發達市場和新興市場，以幫助防範財政風險。這家管理約2.33萬億美元資產的投資機構週二釋出最新的6至12個月週期展望。當前長期美國國債收益率徘徊在2002年以來最高水平附近，其中基準10年期美債收益率約為5.25%，30年期約為5.65%。Pimco經濟學家Tiffany Wilding和全球固定收益首席投資官Andrew Balls在報告中表示，“當前頗具吸引力的初始收益率以及由此帶來的利息收入，可以在相當程度上緩衝通脹尾部風險，同時仍保留債券在人工智慧資本支出推動力減弱或經濟增長遭遇衝擊時發揮對沖作用的潛力。”Pimco表示，投資者“在發達市場和新興市場之間進行全球債券組合配置，有助於分散包括財政風險在內的特定國家風險”。這家資產管理公司表示，美國和法國“尤其突出，兩國的債務路徑更具挑戰性”。與此同時，“英國、義大利和日本仍然較為脆弱 —— 鑑於近期政策進一步擴大赤字，日本的脆弱性有所上升 —— 但按照目前的財政計劃，這些國家的債務路徑看來仍具有可持續性。”

Silver Holds Above $61 as Fed Rate-Hike Bets Fade — Silver hovered around $61 an ounce on Tuesday as fading expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month offset pressure from a firm US dollar and elevated Treasury yields. Traders now see only about a 20% chance of a rate hike this month, while pricing in nearly a 70% probability of an increase in December, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Elsewhere, oil prices fell on Tuesday as resilient Middle Eastern crude exports and a G7 emergency stockpile release eased concerns over potential supply disruptions.

【資深投資人Jim Paulsen稱三重壓力下美股或跌15% 科技股恐下挫20%】美股面對的“憂慮之牆”越高，爬升得似乎就越快，週一收盤距離歷史高點僅一步之遙。不過，在備受關注的資深投資人士Jim Paulsen看來，油價位於每桶100美元、美債收益率達到5%以及美元走強所累積的壓力，尚未真正對市場造成顯著衝擊。Paulsen表示，過去當這三項因素同時對標普500指數構成壓力時，隨後三至五個月內指數最多曾下跌15%。如果這一情形重演，標普500指數今年將自2022年以來首次錄得年度下跌。這將打擊那些押注股市創紀錄漲勢延續的多頭。Paulsen認為，雖然強勁的企業盈利以及圍繞人工智慧的熱情仍在當前水平支撐標普500指數，但收益率、美元和油價上漲帶來的負面影響很快就會顯現。Paulsen說：“我們將迎來一次不小的現實檢驗。重力可能很快再次證明，它對經濟和股市從未失手。”

【派拉蒙擊敗奈飛成功收購華納 好萊塢超級媒體帝國誕生】派拉蒙於當地時間週二完成收購華納兄弟探索公司的交易，科技大亨David Ellison由此掌控龐大媒體帝國，手握好萊塢眾多頂級優質IP。 這筆交易直接將合併後的新公司（命名為天空之舞Skydance）推向行業T1，與奈飛、YouTube這類流媒體巨頭同臺競爭，僅次於迪士尼、亞馬遜等行業巨擘。 Ellison在週二的宣告中表示：“今天是歷史性的一天，不僅對天空之舞，對我們整個行業都是如此。”他補充道：“我們已經迫不及待準備投入工作。” 這家新公司是一番激烈爭奪得來的成果。經過數月拉鋸戰，Ellison擊敗競爭對手奈飛，拿下華納兄弟探索，其間他先後九次提交收購報價。多個州總檢察長組成聯盟曾以反壟斷為由提起訴訟，試圖阻止本次併購，但交易仍落地。

Simandou began production in November last year; Guinea exported 6.8 mln t of iron ore from the concession in 1H. 【摩根士丹利：大選投票後將巴西股票上調至超配】週日總統大選投票後，市場預期巴西將推進財政整頓，摩根士丹利將巴西股票評級上調至超配。弗拉維奧・博索納羅意外領跑，帶動巴西各類資產普漲。分析師尼古拉・利普曼、朱莉婭・諾蓋拉、馬科斯・里奧斯在研報中寫道，他們預計巴西股市到年底最多可上漲 20%；若財政政策轉向，伊博韋斯帕指數有望觸及 25 萬點，2027 年將進一步上行。週一，伊博韋斯帕指數大漲 7.7%，創 2020 年 3 月以來最大單日漲幅。“巴西原有財政模式已經難以為繼，” 分析師稱，“大選開啟了新路徑的可能性：財政整頓疊加國內經濟放緩，最終將擠出債務、吸引投資。”分析師指出，核心在於依託財政整頓，推動市場資金從債市轉向股票與實體投資。本土投資者此前因高利率創紀錄，被擠出股市。巴西股票自由流通市值約 5000 億美元。摩根士丹利估算，本土投資者可新增 300–400 億美元買盤，新興市場投資者可新增 100–200 億美元，全球投資者可新增 300–400 億美元。

### 2026-10-06 20:50:17  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Imports #Importance 1

US Imports Hit Fresh Record High — US imports surged $17.2 billion to an all-time high of $420.8 billion in August 2026, contributing to a widening in the trade deficit to $105.6 billion from a revised $92.8 billion in July. The increase was led by a $9.1 billion rise in industrial supplies and materials, mainly crude oil and nonmonetary gold, while capital goods imports rose $6.2 billion on stronger purchases of semiconductors and other industrial machinery. These gains more than offset a decline in computer accessories. Services imports were broadly unchanged, as higher transport imports offset declines in charges for the use of intellectual property and travel.

US Exports Rise on Higher Industrial Goods Shipments — US exports increased $4.5 billion to $315.2 billion in August 2026, driven by a $6.3 billion rise in industrial supplies and materials, mainly nonmonetary gold and crude oil. Capital goods exports also increased by $1.3 billion, led by semiconductors and computers. These gains were partly offset by a $2.4 billion decline in pharmaceutical exports. Services exports were broadly unchanged, as higher charges for the use of intellectual property and other business services offset declines in travel and financial services.

### 2026-10-06 20:47:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Imports #Importance 1

Canada Imports End Rising Streak — Canada’s goods imports fell 2% month over month to C$73.7 billion in August 2026, marking the first decline since January. Despite the overall decrease, six of 11 product sections recorded gains. Imports of motor vehicles and parts fell 8.8%, while passenger cars and light trucks dropped 15.4% after reaching an all-time high in July due to shorter seasonal production stoppages in the US. Imports of metal and non-metallic mineral products declined 7.0%, led by a 40% drop in unwrought gold, silver and platinum group metals and their alloys, reflecting lower purchases of gold held in Canada by US residents. Imports of metal ores and non-metallic minerals fell 15.5% to their lowest level since November 2025, led by a 17.1% decline in other metal ores and concentrates. Lower imports of lead and zinc ores from Australia and Peru, as well as gold, silver and platinum group metal ores from Brazil, Peru and Mauritania, contributed to the decline.

### 2026-10-06 20:46:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

Canada Trade Surplus Rises to 4-Year High — Canada recorded a trade surplus of C$4.2 billion in August of 2026, widening from the upwardly revised C$0.79 billion in the earlier month to mark the largest trade surplus since May of 2022 and the second-largest since August 2008, despite the appreciation of the Canadian dollar against the greenback. Exports jumped by 2.5% from the previous month to C$77.9 billion as the increase in energy prices due to the war in the Middle East drove energy product sales to surge 4.7%. Exports of consumer goods rose 6.6% and those of industrial machinery and equipment rose 10.1%. Exports to the US surged 8.1% as US consumers likely front-loaded purchases of Canadian goods before a series of tariffs took effect later in the month. Meanwhile, imports fell by 2.0% to C$73.7 billion. Purchases were lower for motor vehicles and parts (-8.8%) and metal and non-metallic mineral products (-7.0%), mainly due to a plunge in unwrought gold, silver, and platinum group metals (-40.0%).

### 2026-10-06 20:37:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Exports #Importance 1

Canada Exports Rebound in August — Canada’s goods exports rose 2.5% month-over-month to C$77.9 billion in August 2026, rebounding from a downwardly revised 2.6% decline in July. Gains were recorded in eight of 11 product sections. Energy exports increased 4.7%, marking the first rise since April. Refined petroleum exports posted the largest monthly gain at 17.4%, driven by higher diesel shipments to Peru, the UK, US and Netherlands. Crude oil exports rose 2.1% on higher prices, while nuclear fuel and other energy product exports jumped 58.4%, driven by increased shipments to Germany and the UK. Consumer goods exports rose 6.6%, led by a 43.3% increase in miscellaneous goods and supplies, partly due to higher shipments of gold and silver coins to the US. Industrial machinery, equipment and parts exports rose 10.1%, their highest level since January 2025, while electronic and electrical equipment and parts increased 11%.

### 2026-10-06 20:36:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Balance of Trade #Importance 3

US Trade Deficit Widens to 17-Month High — The US trade deficit widened to $105.6 billion in August 2026 from a revised $92.8 billion in July, exceeding market expectations of $102.0 billion and marking its largest gap since March 2025. The widening was driven by a $17.2 billion surge in imports to an all-time high of $420.8 billion. Imports of industrial supplies and materials rose $9.1 billion, led by crude oil and nonmonetary gold, while capital goods imports increased $6.2 billion on stronger purchases of semiconductors and other industrial machinery. These gains more than offset a decline in computer accessories. Meanwhile, exports rose $4.5 billion to $315.2 billion, driven by a $6.3 billion increase in industrial supplies and materials, mainly nonmonetary gold and crude oil, and a $1.3 billion rise in capital goods, including semiconductors and computers. Pharmaceutical exports fell $2.4 billion. Exports and imports of services were little changed.

【商務部新聞發言人就法德要求歐盟強化貿易防禦等保護主義工具答記者問 】
問：據媒體報道，10月5日法國和德國向歐委會提交非正式檔案，要求歐盟強化使用貿易防禦措施，並推出多項保護主義新工具。請問中方對此有何評論？
答：中方注意到有關報道。中方始終認為，相互依賴不是風險，利益交融不是威脅，開放合作才是發展的正道。保護主義提升不了競爭力，脫鉤斷鏈只會損人不利己。希望法國和德國作為全球重要經濟體，能夠堅持開放合作和自由貿易，遵守世貿規則，不要鼓動歐盟動輒使用保護主義工具，更不要將經貿問題政治化和泛安全化，避免以錯誤方式走錯誤道路並最終反噬自己。

Mexico Consumer Sentiment Weakens in September — Mexico’s consumer confidence fell 1.2 points to 45.1 in September 2026 from August, retreating from its highest level in a year, while sentiment remained negative. All five components deteriorated on the month. The sharpest decline was in households’ assessment of their current economic situation, which fell 1.9 points to 52. Expectations for households’ economic situation over the next 12 months dropped 1.5 points to 57.6. Assessments of the country’s current economic situation declined 1.3 points to 39, while expectations for the country’s economic outlook fell 0.9 point. Households’ willingness to purchase durable goods, such as furniture and appliances, dropped 0.5 point to 32. Year over year, consumer confidence declined 1.0 point.

郭嘉昆：中國對黃巖島和包括中業島、鐵線礁在內的南沙群島及其附近海域擁有主權，對相關海域擁有主權權利和管轄權。菲律賓的領土範圍是由一系列國際條約確定的，黃巖島和南沙群島不在菲律賓領土範圍之內。近日，菲方飛機和船隻未經中方允許，擅自闖入中國管轄海空域，干擾中方正常執法，嚴重侵犯中國主權和管轄權，蓄意破壞南海和平穩定。中國軍隊和海警依法採取措施應對，正當合理。

中方堅決反對菲律賓損害中國主權和權益的任何行徑，將繼續採取必要措施，堅定捍衛自身在南海的領土主權和海洋權益。中方正告菲方，立即停止侵權挑釁。（外交部官網）

### 2026-10-06 19:41:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Latvia Industrial Output Returns to Growth — Latvia's industrial production rose 0.3% year-on-year in August 2026, rebounding from a 0.2% decline in July. Output growth accelerated in mining and quarrying (36.1% vs 12.4%), while manufacturing rose at a softer pace (7.6% vs 8.2%), as gains eased for food products (8.9% vs 11.7%), fabricated metal products (12.3% vs 33.9%), computer and electronic products (20.4% vs 23.4%) and machinery and equipment (17.5% vs 36.9%), and output fell for beverages (-18.4% vs -5.2%) and motor vehicles (-10.2% vs 11.8%). In contrast, production picked up for repair and installation of machinery (20.2% vs 8.2%) and chemicals (11.5% vs 8.1%). Meanwhile, activity declined at a steeper pace in electricity and gas supply (-44.6% vs -35.6%). On a monthly basis, industrial production rose 0.3% in August, following a 0.1% increase in July. In the first eight months of the year, output increased 4.7% from a year earlier.

### 2026-10-06 19:21:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

10-Year Treasury Yield Softens from 24-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note was at 5.27% on Tuesday, pausing a surge that peaked at a 24-year high of 5.33% touched yesterday, tracking the momentary dip in wholesale fuel prices. Oil bids were tamed by signs that some laden tankers passed through the Strait of Hormuz. The 10-year yield has surged over 110bps this year as risks of higher rates from the Federal Reserve, soaring federal deficit spending, and increasing corporate debt supply triggered a plunge in demand for duration. The price gauge in the ISM Services PMI surged to a four-year high, consolidating expectations that the Fed will deliver another rate hike this year. Meanwhile, a widening budget deficit raised the outlook for the supply of Treasury securities despite the Treasury's preference to service its increasing debt with coupon-less bills. The latest figures from the CBO showed net public debt rising from 101% of GDP this year to over 175% by 2056, assuming a 10-year yield below 4.5%.

【谷歌與Constellation達成為期20年的核電採購協議】谷歌母公司Alphabet Inc.已同意與Constellation Energy Corp.簽署一項為期20年的核電採購協議。在資料中心建設熱潮推動電力需求激增之際，科技公司正競相鎖定長期電力供應。兩家公司週二在宣告中表示，這項協議涉及890兆瓦新增電力，證實了此前的報道。Constellation將為擴建專案投入超過43億美元。新增電力將接入美國最大的輸電運營商PJM Interconnection的電網。人工智慧熱潮需要消耗大量電力，正推動市場重新關注核能。核電既能全天候穩定供電，也不會產生碳排放。近期交易主要圍繞現有核電設施展開，因為新建反應堆從審批到建成通常需要十年甚至更久，而下一代裂變核能技術距離大規模部署可能仍需數年。亞馬遜上週也與Constellation達成類似協議，這家雲及電商公司同意購買690兆瓦電力，其中包括透過升級馬里蘭州Calvert Cliffs核電設施所增加的發電能力。

Sterling Holds Above $1.32 as UK Inflation Concerns Persist — Sterling traded slightly above $1.32 as elevated energy costs and persistent inflation concerns reinforced expectations for UK interest rates to remain higher for longer. Mann has been among a minority on the MPC voting for a 25-bp rate hike to 4% since July. Meanwhile, weaker-than-expected US PCE inflation and jobs data last week strengthened expectations that the Federal Reserve could leave rates unchanged in October, with a December move now seen as more likely. ### 2026-10-06 18:01:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #LMI Logistics Managers Index #Importance 2

US Logistics Growth 2nd-Highest in 4 Years — The US Logistics Managers’ Index increased to 70.2 in September 2026 from 66.6 in August. It is the second-highest reading in four years, behind only June’s reading of 71.1. A faster increase was seen for Inventory Levels (58.9 vs 52.8) and Inventory Costs (79.9 vs 78.6) which is also having a significant effect on the warehousing market. Warehousing Capacity shrank the most since March 2022 (39.3 vs 53.5), potentially due to the fact that firms, particularly retailers, had run inventory down a bit and are not rushing goods in now ahead of potential holiday sales. The reduction in available storage capacity has boosted Warehousing Utilization (64.3 vs 59.6) and is likely behind the consistently robust expansion of Warehousing Prices (73.5 vs 75). There are similar dynamics in Transportation Capacity (34.4 vs 40) while Transportation Utilization continues expanding but at a slower rate (66.1 vs 70.6). Finally, Transportation Prices continue to surge (92.7 vs 90).

In addition, reports showed that crude exports from the Middle East exceeded pre-war levels on four days during the final week of September. Adding to the bearish pressure, G7 countries agreed on Friday to release 100 million barrels of diesel and crude from emergency reserves and pledged to refrain from imposing energy export restrictions. Saudi Arabia also sharply cut the price of its flagship crude grade for Asian buyers as supply flows improved, providing further evidence of a loosening market. Meanwhile, OPEC+ agreed to keep its oil production targets unchanged for November.

Eurozone Retail Sales Inch Higher — Retail sales volumes in the Eurozone inched higher by 0.1% from the previous month in August of 2026, loosely aligned with market expectations of a 0.2% increase and trimming the 0.6% drop in July, which was the sharpest decline in over one year. Sales were sharply higher for core non-food products (0.5% vs -1.2% in July) to reflect some fresh resilience to higher borrowing costs and prices, while food, drinks, and tobacco sales also inched up (0.1% vs -0.1%). In turn, automotive fuel sales volumes plunged 1.9%, extending the 0.2% drop in the earlier period as the surge in fuel prices drove consumers to cut back on tank refills. From the previous year, Eurozone retail sales jumped by 0.8%, rebounding from the two-year low increase of 0.4% in July.

Bund Yields Retreat as ECB Rate-Hike Bets Ease — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield fell to 3.45%, retreating from the 17-year highs reached last week, as turmoil in bond markets prompted investors to scale back expectations for further ECB rate hikes. Markets are now pricing in an 80% probability of another rate increase by year-end. ### 2026-10-06 16:53:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Construction PMI #Importance 1

UK Construction Activity Falls Less in September — The S&P Global UK Construction PMI rose to 46.1 in September 2026 from 44.3 in August, missing expectations of 45.2 and signaling the slowest decline in construction output since January. Commercial construction showed the greatest resilience, falling only marginally, followed by civil engineering and residential construction with softer contractions. However, total new orders fell at the fastest pace since June amid sluggish market conditions, delayed decisions on major projects, and sharply rising input costs. Employment fell again, with job cuts accelerating the fastest in five months. Demand for construction products and materials also declined, while delivery times lengthened to the greatest extent since May amid shipping delays and supply chain disruptions related to the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, input cost inflation eased to a seven-month low. Construction firms remained optimistic about the year ahead, although confidence fell to its lowest since May.

Italy Construction Downturn Eases in September — The S&P Global Italy Construction PMI rose to 46.5 in September 2026, picking up from a four-year low of 41.7 in the previous month. The improvement was driven by a less marked decline in new construction orders, while employment also recorded a modest increase. Meanwhile, lead times continued to lengthen, reflecting the impact of the war in the Middle East, with logistical and transport disruptions and stock shortages weighing on supply-chain performance. On the price front, input-cost inflation accelerated to a four-month high, driven by higher outlays for raw materials, fuel, transport and energy. Among subsectors, all remained in contraction, with the steepest decline recorded in housing, while commercial construction signaled only a slight contraction. Looking ahead, Italian construction companies remained optimistic about output over the next 12 months, although confidence stayed below its long-run average.

### 2026-10-06 15:50:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Construction PMI #Importance 1

French Construction Sector Contracts Less in September — The S&P Global France Construction PMI rose to 39.8 in September 2026 from 37.3 in August, remaining well below the 50 threshold, signaling a continued sector-wide downturn. Commercial construction was once again the weakest segment, posting a steep decline followed by residential construction, while civil engineering activity posted its slowest contraction in eight months. New projects continued to fall, extending the downturn in demand to four-and-a-half years, amid a lack of new tenders, client budget constraints, and general uncertainty. Firms also cut construction material purchases and employment, extending the current period of job shedding that began in May 2024. Meanwhile, input cost inflation remained historically elevated, partly due to repercussions from the war in the Middle East, while delivery delays eased to their lowest since June 2025. French constructors also became more pessimistic about the year ahead, with expectations falling to their weakest since December 2025.

Far-right presidential candidate Marine Le Pen also proposed cutting government spending by €25 billion a year, seeking to strengthen her fiscal credibility as concerns over France’s public finances and bond-market pressures mount. 【Lithuanian parliament backs repeal of constitutional nuclear-weapons ban in first-round vote】Lithuania's parliament on Tuesday approved in a first-round vote a proposal to repeal the constitutional ban on nuclear weapons, the first of two votes required for the change to take effect. The 141-seat parliament recorded 106 votes in favour. The amendment, which would abolish a ban imposed more than 30 years ago, must secure a two-thirds majority in both parliamentary votes; the second round is scheduled for Jan. 12. President Nausėda said parties had agreed the plan in July and that there is currently no plan to host nuclear weapons, but removing the clause would allow deployment if the security situation changes. Lawmakers framed the move as a reassessment of regional security prompted by Russia's actions.

Germany Construction Activity Falls at Faster Pace — The S&P Global Germany Construction PMI Total Activity Index fell to 43.5 in September 2026 from August’s eight-month high of 48.7. Renewed weakness in housing activity was the notable drag, with activity recording its steepest rate of decline since December 2024 after showing signs of steadying in August, while commercial activity also suffered a fresh setback following a brief return to growth. Meanwhile, civil engineering activity fell marginally. New orders continued to decline, albeit with the rate of contraction easing to its weakest in three months. This led constructors to scale back purchases of building materials and products, while the latest reduction in employment was the most pronounced since April. Cost pressures also intensified, with faster increases in both average input costs and subcontractor rates. Looking ahead, constructors remained pessimistic about the outlook for activity over the next 12 months.

Eurozone Construction Activity Remains Weak — The S&P Global Eurozone Construction PMI rose slightly to 43.4 in September 2026 from 43.0 in August, but remained below the 50.0 threshold, signaling a marked contraction in construction activity. The downturn remained broad-based across France, Germany and Italy, with France recording the steepest contraction, Germany seeing a much sharper decline and Italy the slowest fall. Residential construction saw the sharpest contraction and its fastest decline since February 2025, led by Germany. Meanwhile, new orders continued to fall, extending the downturn in demand to four-and-a-half years, although the pace eased from August. 【華為徐直軍：Peerium架構與靈衢匯流排 讓百萬顆處理器成為一臺計算機】在上海舉行的華為全聯接（HC）大會期間，華為輪值董事長徐直軍與海思首席科學家廖恆，就華為新發布的AI時代的Peerium計算架構和靈衢匯流排（UnifiedBus），與媒體記者進行了問答交流。 今年HC大會期間，華為展出了昇騰950和Atlas 950超節點。徐直軍表示，“超節點”如今被廣泛提及，但不同廠商實現差異很大，真正的挑戰在於讓數千、數萬顆處理器組成一臺計算機。華為將這一全新計算架構命名為Peerium，它能讓百萬顆處理器成為一臺計算機。目前，華為正部署和測試25.6萬張卡規模的Atlas 950 SuperPoD超節點。 廖恆認為，未來兩年內，預計中國將出現約6至7個前沿AI實驗室，目標都是訓練引數規模在10萬億到40萬億之間的基礎語言模型。這些數字與超節點的記憶體容量和規模大致匹配，因此需要如此規模的基礎設施來做訓練。（科創板日報）

Energy production recorded the strongest growth, rising by 4.7%, followed by intermediate goods (2%) and capital goods (0.9%). By contrast, consumer goods production declined by 1%. Durable consumer goods was the only sector to post positive monthly growth, at 1%, while energy recorded the sharpest decline, falling by 2%.

【CHINA STATE PLANNER: six-network initiative to open major projects to private firms】Li Hui, director of the Private Economy Development Bureau at CHINA STATE PLANNER, said converting new capacity from the six-network initiative into real returns requires three actions. First, broaden access: establish long-term mechanisms for private participation in major projects and open competitive infrastructure sectors fairly to operators of different sizes and specializations. Second, improve retention: address private firms’ concerns on return mechanisms and competition by rolling out projects with stable operating returns suitable for private capital, enhancing financing channels, strengthening factor support, and removing market-access, standards-alignment and information-sharing bottlenecks. 敞開大門，讓民營企業“進得去”。我們正在完善民營企業參與重大專案建設長效機制，推進基礎設施競爭性領域向經營主體公平開放。不同體量、專長的企業，都能在這張網上找到自己的位置。

### 2026-10-06 15:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

France Budget Deficit Widens in Jan-Aug — France’s state budget deficit widened to EUR 159.6 billion in the January-August period from EUR 157.5 billion a year earlier. Expenditure rose 4.4% year-on-year to EUR 362.97 billion, driven mainly by higher debt-servicing costs amid rising interest rates and inflation, increased military spending, and higher public energy service charges (CSPE). Meanwhile, general budget revenue increased 3.1% to EUR 228.55 billion. Net tax revenue rose to EUR 206 billion, supported by higher net personal income tax, the State’s share of net VAT revenue, net corporate income tax, and other net tax revenue. Non-tax revenue increased to EUR 18.9 billion, mainly due to the return to the State in January 2026 of EUR 6.9 billion in non-consumable endowments allocated under the Future Investment Programs (PIA) in 2010 and 2014, which had reached maturity. Special Treasury accounts posted a EUR 25.2 billion deficit, an improvement of EUR 6.3 billion from the end of August 2025.

### 2026-10-06 15:16:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Hungary Retail Sales Growth Slows to Over 1-Year Low — Retail sales in Hungary rose by 2.4% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 4.9% increase in the previous month. This reading marked the weakest growth since May 2025, as sales moderated in food products (0.5% vs 2.2% in July), with lower activity in non-specialized food and beverages (1.9% vs 3.6%) and a continued decline in food, beverages and tobacco (-4.8% vs -2.2%). Sales of non-food products also slowed (5.0% vs 6.5%), including manufactured goods in non-specialized shops (4.2% vs 5.1%), textiles, clothing and footwear (2.7% vs 5.4%), furniture and electrical goods (1.0% vs 5.2%), books, computer equipment and other goods (0.6% vs 5.9%), and pharmaceutical and medical goods and cosmetic articles (5.4% vs 9.2%). Automotive fuel sales also rose at a softer pace (3.1% vs 8.1%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade fell by 0.7% in August, following a 1.0% rise in the previous month.

Switzerland Jobless Rate Steady at 3% — Switzerland’s non-seasonally adjusted unemployment rate was 3.0% in September 2026, unchanged from the previous two months. The number of unemployed people fell by 1,064 from a month earlier to 140,480. Across the cantons, the largest decreases were recorded in Zurich (-355), St. Gallen (-224), Aargau (-204), Freiburg (-185), and Waadt (-147). At the same time, deflation eased for food and soft drinks (-3.5% vs -4.3%). Meanwhile, inflation eased slightly for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (4.6% vs 4.7%). ### 2026-10-06 15:06:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 1

Austria Wholesale Inflation Highest Since 2023 — Wholesale prices in Austria climbed by 10.2% year-on-year in September 2026, marking the fastest rise since February 2023 and accelerating from an 8.2% gain in the previous month. The increase was driven by sharp price gains in other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (68.8%), motor spirit (45.1%), plastics and rubber in primary forms (26.6%), waste and scrap (25.6%), and non-iron metals (21.2%). Prices also rose for information and communication equipment (18.8%), other machinery and equipment (18.1%), solid fuels (16.2%), grain, seeds and animal feeds (11.8%), and iron and steel (11.8%). In contrast, prices fell for live animals (-10.9%) and dairy products, eggs and edible oils and fats (-1.3%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices rose 1.9% in September, the highest in six months and accelerating from 0.9% in August, mainly due to higher prices for other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (12.6%), motor spirit (10.4%), and grain, seeds and animal feeds (7.0%).

### 2026-10-06 15:02:20  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Gains Capped by Dollar Strength, External Risks — The Indonesian rupiah hovered around IDR 17,910 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, edging higher from the previous session as traders awaited key domestic releases later this week, including September forex reserves, consumer sentiment, and August retail sales. Meanwhile, fiscal conditions through August were supported by revenue growth, even as tax-restitution delays reportedly persisted. The upside remained limited, however, as Indonesia's external position stayed vulnerable, with elevated imports and energy costs weighing on the balance amid persistent Middle East headwinds. Inflation could also become harder to contain in the coming months as food prices stay elevated following supply disruptions linked to El Niño. On the global front, the dollar index lingered near an 18-month high, lifted by elevated U.S. Treasury yields and higher oil prices, while euro weakness amid political and fiscal concerns in the eurozone further boosted demand for the greenback, capping further rupiah gains.

### 2026-10-06 14:57:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 2

France Industrial Output Surprisingly Falls — Industrial production in France fell 0.3% month-on-month in August 2026, defying expectations for a 0.3% rise and following a 0.6% decline in July. The drop was mainly driven by a 2.4% decline in extractive industries, energy and water, which more than offset a 0.3% increase in manufacturing. The energy decline was largely due to lower electricity production after output reached its highest level since December 2021 in July. Within manufacturing, output of other industrial products rose 1.2%, while electrical, electronic and computer equipment increased 0.9%. Coking and refining output also rose 3.3%. In contrast, transport equipment manufacturing fell 2.2%, while agri-food production dropped 1.2%. On a yearly basis, industrial output rose 0.3%, rebounding from a 0.6% fall in July. Over the three months to August, industrial production was down 0.1% from a year earlier.

### 2026-10-06 14:57:19  #公司 #市場

【Reflection AI正式釋出Beam 對標主流開源模型】Reflection AI正式推出其首款前沿開源權重模型Beam。這家成立兩年、總部位於布魯克林的初創企業稱，Beam在高階推理基準測試上效能對標主流開源模型，同時成本大幅降低。企業週一在一篇長篇部落格中披露更多細節，稱Beam為純文字混合專家模型，基於高算力強化學習訓練，在推理、程式碼編寫與智慧體任務上具備出色能力，“token成本與推理算力僅為競品的一小部分”。Beam總引數量5010億，啟用引數量230億。預訓練使用23.8萬億token，上下文視窗達100萬token。Reflection宣稱的效能尚未經過獨立驗證，但該公司表示，在高階推理基準測試中，Beam得分與Z.ai的GLM-5.2相當，且優於當前主流西方開源模型，同時推理算力消耗“低3至4倍”。Reflection將其定位為面向企業、公共部門與開發者的“主力模型”。

### 2026-10-06 14:40:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Taiwan #Stock Market #Importance 1

TAIEX Ends at Record High, Nears 49,900 — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, rose 111 points, or 0.2%, to a fresh record peak of 49,823 on Tuesday, extending gains for a fifth consecutive session, buoyed by gains in financials, process industries, and technology services. The broader index closed above the 49,000 level for the second straight session, tracking an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight, driven by a rally in tech stocks. Traders sought to capitalize on the momentum, with the financial sector rising 1.2%, mainly supported by gains in Fubon Financial Holding (1.6%), Cathay Financial Holding (0.5%), and CTBC Financial Holding (1.5%). Meanwhile, process industries climbed 1.4%, with Nan Ya Plastics Corp. rising 2.5%, while technology services surged 3.5%, boosted by a 6.5% jump in Wiwynn Corp. By contrast, electronic technology edged down 0.1%; however, TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, rose 0.4%.

### 2026-10-06 14:38:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Hungary Industrial Output Growth Accelerates in August — Industrial production in Hungary grew by 8.7% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from a 4.7% rise in the previous month. The increase was driven primarily by the two largest manufacturing sub-sectors, such as vehicle manufacturing and computer, electronic and optical product manufacturing. However, production declined across most other manufacturing sub-sectors, including the manufacture of electrical equipment and food, beverage and tobacco products. On a seasonally and working-day adjusted monthly basis, industrial output rose by 1.0% in August, following a 1.8% gain in the preceding period. For the January to August period, industrial activity was 3.4% higher than in the corresponding period of the previous year.

### 2026-10-06 14:28:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Manufacturing Production MoM #Importance 1

Danish Manufacturing Output Rebounds in August — Denmark’s manufacturing production rose by 0.9% month-on-month in August 2026, rebounding from an upwardly revised eighteen-month low of a 5.0% drop in the previous month. Output recovered in textiles, clothing, and leather products (6.4% vs -3.8% in July), wood and paper products (3.7% vs -1.6%), petroleum and chemical products (7.9% vs -2.0%), metal and metal products (4.4% vs -12.0%), electronics (3.4% vs -13.6%), and transportation (39.0% vs -17.8%). Moreover, activity increased in the manufacture of electrical equipment (3.8% vs 2.3%) and rubber, plastic, glass, and concrete (2.9% vs 0.3%). Output also declined at a softer pace for food, beverage, and tobacco (-0.6% vs -2.4%) and pharmaceuticals (-1.5% vs -6.0%). On a non-seasonally adjusted annual basis, manufacturing production grew by 6.5% in August, following a downwardly revised 3.4% increase in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-06 14:15:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Factory Orders #Importance 2

German Factory Orders Slump on Large-Order Reversal — Germany’s factory orders plunged 10.6% month-on-month in August 2026, reversing an upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the prior month and far exceeding market expectations for a 1.0% decline. It was the first monthly drop since April, largely reflecting a 61.5% tumble in orders for aircraft, ships, trains and military vehicles after these surged more than twofold in July on an exceptionally high volume of large contracts. Demand weakened across capital goods (-15.3%), intermediate goods (-2.6%) and consumer goods (-7.3%). Domestic orders fell 17.3%, while foreign demand declined 5.4%, with orders from both the euro area (-5.4%) and non-euro area countries (-5.5%) decreasing. Excluding large-scale contracts, orders edged down just 0.1%. On a less volatile three-month basis, factory orders fell 1.3% in June-August from the previous three months, or 2.6% excluding large orders, pointing to continued weakness in underlying industrial demand.

### 2026-10-06 14:10:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Romania Retail Sales Rebound After Yearlong Decline — Romania’s retail sales increased 0.1% year-on-year in August 2026, marking the first increase after twelve consecutive months of decline, compared with a 6.1% drop in the previous month. The rebound was led by non-food product sales, which increased (0.2% vs -6.7% in July), while sales of food, beverages and tobacco declined (-1.9% vs -6.0%) and retail sales of automotive fuel in specialized stores also fell (-0.3% vs -4.8%), albeit at a softer pace. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade rose 2.3%, accelerating from a 0.8% increase in the previous month. In the first eight months of the year, retail sales declined 5.1% from the same period a year earlier.
